1	# Coding Conventions
2	
3	**Analysis Date:** 2026-03-27
4	
5	## TypeScript Configuration
6	
7	**Base Config:** `tsconfig.base.json` (all packages/apps extend this)
8	
9	```json
10	{
11	  "target": "ES2022",
12	  "module": "ESNext",
13	  "moduleResolution": "bundler",
14	  "strict": true,
15	  "strictNullChecks": true,
16	  "isolatedModules": true,
17	  "noUnusedLocals": true,
18	  "noUnusedParameters": true,
19	  "noImplicitReturns": true,
20	  "noFallthroughCasesInSwitch": true,
21	  "declaration": true,
22	  "declarationMap": true
23	}
24	```
25	
26	**Per-workspace overrides:**
27	
28	| Workspace      | Module             | moduleResolution | Extras                                             |
29	| -------------- | ------------------ | ---------------- | -------------------------------------------------- |
30	| `packages/*`   | ESNext (from base) | bundler          | `outDir: ./dist`, `rootDir: ./src`                 |
31	| `apps/api`     | CommonJS           | node             | `emitDecoratorMetadata`, `experimentalDecorators`  |
32	| `apps/web`     | ESNext             | bundler          | `jsx: react-jsx`, `noEmit: true`, `target: ES2020` |
33	| `apps/desktop` | ESNext             | bundler          | Extends base                                       |
34	
35	## Naming Patterns
36	
37	### Files
38	
39	**Backend (`apps/api/src/`):** kebab-case with NestJS suffix convention:
40	
41	- Controllers: `vault.controller.ts`
42	- Services: `vault.service.ts`
43	- Modules: `vault.module.ts`
44	- DTOs: `init-vault.dto.ts`, `create-share.dto.ts`
45	- Entities: `vault.entity.ts`, `pinned-cid.entity.ts`
46	- Guards: `jwt-auth.guard.ts`, `throttler-bypass.guard.ts`
47	- Strategies: `jwt.strategy.ts`
48	- Decorators: `allow-scope.decorator.ts`
49	- Unit tests: `vault.service.spec.ts` (co-located)
50	
51	**Frontend (`apps/web/src/`):** PascalCase for components, camelCase for hooks/services:
52	
53	- Components: `FileBrowser.tsx`, `ConfirmDialog.tsx`, `AppShell.tsx`
54	- Hooks: `useFolder.ts`, `useSyncPolling.ts`, `useFileDownload.ts`
55	- Stores: `folder.store.ts`, `auth.store.ts`, `upload.store.ts`
56	- Services: `upload.service.ts`, `folder.service.ts`, `ipns.service.ts`
57	- Utilities: `fileTypes.ts`, `format.ts`
58	- CSS modules: `file-browser.css`, `layout.css` (per-component, not CSS modules)
59	
60	**Packages (`packages/*/src/`):** kebab-case, organized by domain:
61	
62	- Module entry: `index.ts` (barrel re-exports)
63	- Domain dirs: `aes/`, `ecies/`, `folder/`, `file/`
64	- Within domains: `encrypt.ts`, `decrypt.ts`, `types.ts`, `metadata.ts`
65	- Tests: `__tests__/` directory at package root
66	
67	**Rust (`crates/*/src/`, `apps/desktop/src-tauri/src/`):** snake_case per Rust convention:
68	
69	- Module files: `mod.rs` for directories, `lib.rs` for crate root
70	- Feature files: `inode.rs`, `cache.rs`, `file_handle.rs`
71	- Error types: `error.rs` in every crate
72	- Platform-specific: `platform/windows/`, `platform/macos/` (feature-gated)
73	
74	### Directories
75	
76	**Backend domain modules:** singular nouns: `auth/`, `vault/`, `ipfs/`, `ipns/`, `shares/`, `tee/`
77	
78	**Backend sub-dirs within modules:** plural nouns: `dto/`, `entities/`, `guards/`, `services/`, `strategies/`, `decorators/`
79	
80	**Frontend component groups:** feature-based: `file-browser/`, `layout/`, `auth/`, `settings/`, `mfa/`, `ui/`
81	
82	### Functions and Variables
83	
84	- **Functions:** camelCase everywhere in TypeScript: `encryptAesGcm`, `fetchAndDecryptMetadata`, `createSubfolder`
85	- **Variables:** camelCase: `privateKey`, `folderKey`, `rootIpnsName`
86	- **Constants:** UPPER_SNAKE_CASE: `AES_KEY_SIZE`, `QUOTA_LIMIT_BYTES`, `ROOT_INO`
87	- **Unused params:** prefix with underscore: `_ctx`, `_error`, `_removed`
88	
89	### Types
90	
91	- **Use `type` keyword** (not `interface`) for data shapes. Interfaces only for class contracts (e.g., `RequestWithUser extends Request`).
92	- **PascalCase** for all types: `FolderMetadata`, `VaultKey`, `SdkContext`, `CipherBoxClientConfig`
93	- **Suffixes by purpose:**
94	  - Data shapes: `Entry`, `Metadata`, `Config`, `State`, `Result`
95	  - DTOs: suffixed `Dto` (`InitVaultDto`, `CreateShareDto`, `QuotaResponseDto`)
96	  - Events: domain:action pattern (`SdkEvent` union type)
97	
98	### String Literal Unions Over Enums
99	
100	**Never use TypeScript `enum`**. The codebase has zero enum declarations. Use string literal union types:
101	
102	```typescript
103	// Correct
104	export type CryptoErrorCode =
105	  | 'ENCRYPTION_FAILED'
106	  | 'DECRYPTION_FAILED'
107	  | 'KEY_WRAPPING_FAILED';
108	
109	export type DeviceAuthStatus = 'pending' | 'approved' | 'denied';
110	
111	// Wrong -- never do this
112	export enum CryptoErrorCode { ... }
113	```
114	
115	### API Fields vs Database Columns
116	
117	**API/TypeScript:** camelCase for all fields:
118	
119	```typescript
120	// DTO (camelCase)
121	ownerPublicKey!: string;
122	rootIpnsName!: string;
123	encryptedKey!: Buffer;
124	```
125	
126	**Database columns:** snake_case via TypeORM `name` option:
127	
128	```typescript
129	// Entity (property camelCase, column snake_case)
130	@Column({ type: 'uuid', name: 'owner_id' })
131	ownerId!: string;
132	
133	@Column({ type: 'bytea', name: 'owner_public_key' })
134	ownerPublicKey!: Buffer;
135	
136	@CreateDateColumn({ name: 'created_at' })
137	createdAt!: Date;
138	```
139	
140	## Code Style
141	
142	### Formatting
143	
144	**Tool:** Prettier 3.x (root-level dependency, no `.prettierrc` file -- uses defaults)
145	
146	- 2-space indentation
147	- Single quotes for strings
148	- Semicolons required
149	- Trailing commas (Prettier default: `all` in v3)
150	- 100-char print width (default)
151	
152	### Linting
153	
154	**Tool:** ESLint 9.x with flat config (`eslint.config.js`)
155	
156	```javascript
157	// Key rules:
158	'@typescript-eslint/no-unused-vars': ['error', { argsIgnorePattern: '^_' }]
159	'@typescript-eslint/explicit-function-return-type': 'off'
160	'@typescript-eslint/no-explicit-any': 'warn'  // warn, not error
161	```
162	
163	**Plugins:** `@eslint/js`, `typescript-eslint`, `eslint-plugin-prettier`
164	
165	**No Biome:** Despite the web app CLAUDE.md referencing "Biome lint", the repo has no `biome.json`. JSX lint rules (e.g., `noCommentText`) are from the web app's review process, not an active Biome config.
166	
167	### Pre-commit Hooks
168	
169	**husky + lint-staged** enforced on every commit:
170	
171	1. `scripts/check-api-client.sh` -- blocks commits that modify `.dto.ts`, `.controller.ts`, or `.entity.ts` files without also staging regenerated `packages/api-client/openapi.json`. Fix: run `pnpm api:generate`.
172	2. `lint-staged` runs:
173	   - `*.{ts,tsx,js,jsx}` -> `eslint --fix` + `prettier --write`
174	   - `*.{json,yml,yaml}` -> `prettier --write`
175	   - `*.md` -> `markdownlint --fix` + `prettier --write`
176	
177	### Commit Messages
178	
179	**commitlint** enforces Conventional Commits via husky `commit-msg` hook:
180	
181	```text
182	type(optional-scope): description
183	```
184	
185	Valid types: `feat`, `fix`, `docs`, `style`, `refactor`, `perf`, `test`, `build`, `ci`, `chore`, `revert`
186	
187	**Custom rule:** Subject must not contain parenthesized text -- Release Please misparses it as a scope. Use dashes or brackets instead.
188	
189	```bash
190	# Correct
191	feat(api): add vault export endpoint
192	chore: remove unused config
193	
194	# Wrong -- will be rejected by commitlint
195	fix: update handler (legacy)  # parens in subject
196	```
197	
198	## Import Organization
199	
200	### Order
201	
202	1. External framework imports (`react`, `@nestjs/*`, `typeorm`, `zustand`, `tauri`)
203	2. Monorepo package imports (`@cipherbox/crypto`, `@cipherbox/core`, `@cipherbox/sdk-core`, `@cipherbox/sdk`, `@cipherbox/api-client`)
204	3. Local absolute imports (from `../` or `./`)
205	4. CSS imports (last, in `.tsx` files)
206	
207	### Example (Backend Controller)
208	
209	```typescript
210	import { Controller, Post, Get, Body, UseGuards, Request } from '@nestjs/common';
211	import { ApiTags, ApiOperation, ApiResponse, ApiBearerAuth } from '@nestjs/swagger';
212	import { JwtAuthGuard } from '../auth/guards/jwt-auth.guard';
213	import { VaultService } from './vault.service';
214	import { InitVaultDto, VaultResponseDto } from './dto/init-vault.dto';
215	import { RequestWithUser } from '../common/types';
216	```
217	
218	### Example (Frontend Component)
219	
220	```typescript
221	import { useState, useCallback, useEffect } from 'react';
222	import type { FolderChild, FilePointer } from '@cipherbox/core';
223	import { useFolderNavigation } from '../../hooks/useFolderNavigation';
224	import { useFolder } from '../../hooks/useFolder';
225	import { useFolderStore } from '../../stores/folder.store';
226	import { FileList } from './FileList';
227	import '../../styles/file-browser.css';
228	```
229	
230	### Path Aliases
231	
232	- No path aliases configured. Use relative imports everywhere.
233	- Monorepo packages are imported by package name: `@cipherbox/crypto`, `@cipherbox/core`, etc.
234	
235	### Barrel Files
236	
237	**All packages use `index.ts` barrel exports:**
238	
239	- `packages/crypto/src/index.ts` -- re-exports all public crypto functions and types
240	- `packages/core/src/index.ts` -- re-exports folder, file, vault, IPNS types and functions
241	- `packages/sdk-core/src/index.ts` -- re-exports IPFS, IPNS, folder, file, upload, download ops
242	- `packages/sdk/src/index.ts` -- re-exports `CipherBoxClient`, events, types
243	- `packages/api-client/src/index.ts` -- re-exports generated API functions and models
244	
245	**Within packages, sub-modules also have barrel files:**
246	
247	- `packages/crypto/src/aes/index.ts` re-exports `encrypt`, `decrypt`, `seal`, `unseal`
248	- `packages/core/src/folder/index.ts` re-exports types and metadata functions
249	
250	**Backend entities and DTOs use barrel files:**
251	
252	- `apps/api/src/vault/dto/index.ts`
253	- `apps/api/src/vault/entities/index.ts`
254	- `apps/api/src/shares/entities/index.ts`
255	
256	**Frontend component groups use barrel files:**
257	
258	- `apps/web/src/components/file-browser/index.ts` -- selectively exports main components
259	- `apps/web/src/components/layout/index.ts` -- exports all layout components
260	
261	## NestJS Backend Conventions (`apps/api`)
262	
263	### Module Structure
264	
265	Every domain is a NestJS module with this structure:
266	
267	```text
268	apps/api/src/{domain}/
269	  {domain}.module.ts       # Module declaration
270	  {domain}.controller.ts   # HTTP endpoints
271	  {domain}.service.ts      # Business logic
272	  {domain}.controller.spec.ts
273	  {domain}.service.spec.ts
274	  dto/
275	    index.ts               # Barrel export
276	    {action}-{entity}.dto.ts
277	  entities/
278	    index.ts               # Barrel export
279	    {entity}.entity.ts
280	```
281	
282	### Controller Pattern
283	
284	```typescript
285	@ApiTags('Vault')
286	@ApiBearerAuth()
287	@UseGuards(JwtAuthGuard)
288	@Controller('vault')
289	export class VaultController {
290	  constructor(private readonly vaultService: VaultService) {}
291	
292	  @Post('init')
293	  @ApiOperation({ summary: '...', description: '...' })
294	  @ApiResponse({ status: 201, description: '...', type: VaultResponseDto })
295	  @ApiResponse({ status: 401, description: 'Unauthorized' })
296	  @ApiResponse({ status: 409, description: 'Conflict' })
297	  async initializeVault(
298	    @Request() req: RequestWithUser,
299	    @Body() dto: InitVaultDto
300	  ): Promise<VaultResponseDto> {
301	    return this.vaultService.initializeVault(req.user.id, dto);
302	  }
303	}
304	```
305	
306	**Rules:**
307	
308	- Always use `@ApiTags`, `@ApiBearerAuth`, `@ApiOperation`, `@ApiResponse` decorators
309	- Inject services via `private readonly` constructor params
310	- Extract `userId` from `req.user.id` (typed via `RequestWithUser`)
311	- Controllers delegate to services -- no business logic in controllers
312	- Return typed DTOs from all endpoints
313	
314	### Service Pattern
315	
316	```typescript
317	@Injectable()
318	export class VaultService {
319	  constructor(
320	    @InjectRepository(Vault) private readonly vaultRepository: Repository<Vault>,
321	    private readonly configService: ConfigService
322	  ) {}
323	}
324	```
325	
326	**Rules:**
327	
328	- All services are `@Injectable()`
329	- Use `@InjectRepository` for TypeORM repositories
330	- Throw NestJS exceptions (`ConflictException`, `NotFoundException`, `BadRequestException`)
331	- JSDoc comments on all public methods
332	
333	### DTO Pattern
334	
335	```typescript
336	export class InitVaultDto {
337	  @ApiProperty({ description: '...', example: '...' })
338	  @IsString()
339	  @IsNotEmpty()
340	  @Matches(/^[0-9a-fA-F]+$/, { message: '...' })
341	  ownerPublicKey!: string;
342	}
343	```
344	
345	**Rules:**
346	
347	- Use `class-validator` decorators for request DTOs
348	- Use `@ApiProperty` for Swagger documentation on all fields
349	- Use definite assignment assertion (`!:`) on all DTO fields
350	- Response DTOs need `@ApiProperty` but not validation decorators
351	
352	### Entity Pattern
353	
354	```typescript
355	@Entity('vaults') // table name is plural, snake_case
356	export class Vault {
357	  @PrimaryGeneratedColumn('uuid')
358	  id!: string;
359	
360	  @Column({ type: 'uuid', name: 'owner_id' })
361	  ownerId!: string;
362	
363	  @ManyToOne(() => User, { onDelete: 'CASCADE' })
364	  @JoinColumn({ name: 'owner_id' })
365	  owner!: User;
366	
367	  @CreateDateColumn({ name: 'created_at' })
368	  createdAt!: Date;
369	}
370	```
371	
372	**Rules:**
373	
374	- Table names: plural snake_case (`vaults`, `pinned_cids`, `folder_ipns`)
375	- Column names: explicit snake_case via `name` option
376	- Property names: camelCase
377	- Binary data stored as `bytea` type, typed as `Buffer`
378	- UUIDs as primary keys via `@PrimaryGeneratedColumn('uuid')`
379	- Definite assignment assertions (`!:`) on all fields
380	- `onDelete: 'CASCADE'` on foreign key relations
381	
382	## React Frontend Conventions (`apps/web`)
383	
384	### Component Pattern
385	
386	```typescript
387	type ConfirmDialogProps = {
388	  open: boolean;
389	  onClose: () => void;
390	  onConfirm: () => void;
391	  title: string;
392	  message: string;
393	  confirmLabel?: string;
394	  isDestructive?: boolean;
395	  isLoading?: boolean;
396	};
397	
398	/**
399	 * JSDoc with description and @example block.
400	 */
401	export function ConfirmDialog({ open, onClose, onConfirm, ... }: ConfirmDialogProps) {
402	  // ...
403	}
404	```
405	
406	**Rules:**
407	
408	- Use `function` declarations (not arrow functions) for components
409	- Props type defined as `type XxxProps` above the component
410	- Named exports, not default exports
411	- JSDoc with `@example` on major components
412	- CSS via imported stylesheets, not CSS modules or CSS-in-JS
413	
414	### Zustand Store Pattern
415	
416	```typescript
417	import { create } from 'zustand';
418	
419	type FolderState = {
420	  // State fields
421	  folders: Record<string, FolderNode>;
422	  currentFolderId: string | null;
423	  // Action signatures
424	  setFolder: (folder: FolderNode) => void;
425	  clearFolders: () => void;
426	};
427	
428	export const useFolderStore = create<FolderState>((set, get) => ({
429	  // Initial state
430	  folders: {},
431	  currentFolderId: null,
432	
433	  // Actions (inline)
434	  setFolder: (folder) =>
435	    set((state) => ({
436	      folders: { ...state.folders, [folder.id]: folder },
437	    })),
438	
439	  clearFolders: () => {
440	    /* ... */
441	  },
442	}));
443	```
444	
445	**Rules:**
446	
447	- Store files named `{domain}.store.ts`
448	- State type includes both data fields and action signatures
449	- Use `set` for state updates, `get` for reading current state
450	- Immutable updates via spread operator
451	- **Security:** Zero-fill `Uint8Array` key material in cleanup actions
452	- **Stale closures:** Inside async callbacks, use `useFolderStore.getState()` not hook selectors
453	
454	### Hook Pattern
455	
456	```typescript
457	/**
458	 * JSDoc with @example showing usage in JSX.
459	 */
460	export function useFolder() {
461	  const folderMutations = useFolderMutations();
462	  const fileOperations = useFileOperations();
463	
464	  const isLoading = folderMutations.isLoading || fileOperations.isLoading;
465	  const error = folderMutations.error || fileOperations.error;
466	
467	  return {
468	    isLoading,
469	    error,
470	    createFolder: folderMutations.createFolder,
471	    // ...
472	  };
473	}
474	```
475	
476	**Rules:**
477	
478	- Hook files named `use{Feature}.ts`
479	- Compose smaller hooks into larger facade hooks
480	- Return object with named properties (not tuples)
481	- Combine loading/error state from sub-hooks
482	
483	### Routing
484	
485	- `react-router-dom` with `HashRouter` (required for Tauri webview)
486	- Route pages in `apps/web/src/routes/`: `FilesPage.tsx`, `BinPage.tsx`, etc.
487	- Protected routes: `useEffect` redirect to `/` when `!isAuthenticated`
488	- Layout via `AppShell` wrapper component
489	
490	### CSS
491	
492	- Plain CSS files per component group in `apps/web/src/styles/`
493	- Modern color function notation: `rgb(0 0 0 / 50%)` not `rgba(0,0,0,0.5)`
494	- All interactive elements must have `:focus-visible` styles alongside `:hover`
495	
496	### Accessibility
497	
498	- ARIA roles require matching keyboard handlers (`role="button"` needs `onKeyDown` for Enter/Space)
499	- Remove `tabIndex` if keyboard interaction is not needed
500	- See `apps/web/CLAUDE.md` for full a11y checklist
501	
502	## Package Layer Conventions
503	
504	### `@cipherbox/crypto` -- Pure Cryptographic Primitives
505	
506	- No CipherBox domain knowledge; generic crypto operations only
507	- All inputs/outputs are `Uint8Array`
508	- Error messages are generic to prevent oracle attacks
509	- Custom `CryptoError` class with typed `CryptoErrorCode` string literal union
510	- Web Crypto API for AES operations; `@noble/*` and `eciesjs` for ECC
511	
512	### `@cipherbox/core` -- Domain Types and Metadata
513	
514	- Knows CipherBox data model (FolderMetadata, FileMetadata, DeviceRegistry)
515	- Imports from `@cipherbox/crypto` only
516	- Types use `type` keyword, not `interface`
517	- Validation functions: `validateFolderMetadata`, `validateFileMetadata`
518	- Encrypt/decrypt functions pair: `encryptFolderMetadata` / `decryptFolderMetadata`
519	
520	### `@cipherbox/sdk-core` -- Stateless Operations
521	
522	- Pure functions taking `SdkContext` as first argument (dependency injection)
523	- No global state; no Zustand; no React
524	- `SdkContext` provides `apiUrl`, `getAccessToken()`, and optional `axiosInstance`
525	
526	### `@cipherbox/sdk` -- Stateful Client
527	
528	- `CipherBoxClient` class with internal state (`FolderTree`, `KeyCache`)
529	- Event-driven via `SdkEventEmitter` with typed `SdkEvent` union
530	- Zero React/browser dependencies
531	- Defensive copy of key material in constructor; zeroed on `destroy()`
532	- Operations wrapped with `withOperation()` for consistent start/end/error events
533	
534	### `@cipherbox/api-client` -- Generated API Client
535	
536	- Auto-generated by Orval from OpenAPI spec
537	- **Never edit `src/generated/` or `src/models/` manually**
538	- Custom axios instance in `src/instance.ts` (the only hand-written file)
539	- Regenerate after any API change: `pnpm api:generate`
540	
541	## Rust Conventions (`crates/*`, `apps/desktop/src-tauri/`)
542	
543	### Module Structure
544	
545	```text
546	crates/{name}/src/
547	  lib.rs          # Public API re-exports
548	  error.rs        # Crate-specific error enum
549	  {feature}.rs    # Feature modules
550	```
551	
552	### Error Handling
553	
554	Use `thiserror` derive macro for error enums in every crate:
555	
556	```rust
557	#[derive(Debug, Error)]
558	pub enum CryptoError {
559	    #[error("AES-GCM encryption failed")]
560	    AesEncryptionFailed,
561	    #[error("Invalid key size: expected {expected}, got {actual}")]
562	    InvalidKeySize { expected: usize, actual: usize },
563	}
564	```
565	
566	**Composition:** Higher-level crates use `#[from]` for automatic conversion:
567	
568	```rust
569	#[derive(Debug, Error)]
570	pub enum SdkError {
571	    #[error("Crypto error: {0}")]
572	    Crypto(#[from] cipherbox_crypto::CryptoError),
573	    #[error("API error: {0}")]
574	    Api(#[from] cipherbox_api_client::ApiError),
575	}
576	```
577	
578	**Tauri commands** return `Result<T, String>` (Tauri's IPC serialization constraint). Map errors with `.map_err(|e| format!("...: {}", e))`.
579	
580	### Unsafe Usage
581	
582	Minimal and isolated. Only used for:
583	
584	- `libc::getuid()` / `libc::getgid()` in FUSE operations (`crates/fuse/src/operations.rs`)
585	- WinFSP raw pointer operations (`crates/fuse/src/platform/windows/read_ops.rs`)
586	
587	No `unsafe` in application code (`apps/desktop/src-tauri/src/`), crypto, core, SDK, or API client crates.
588	
589	### Key Material
590	
591	- `Zeroizing<Vec<u8>>` from the `zeroize` crate for automatic zeroing on drop
592	- Private keys stored as `Zeroizing<Vec<u8>>`, never as raw `Vec<u8>`
593	
594	### Naming
595	
596	- snake_case for everything per Rust convention
597	- Crate names: `cipherbox_crypto`, `cipherbox_core`, `cipherbox_sdk`, `cipherbox_fuse`, `cipherbox_api_client`
598	- Type names: PascalCase (`CryptoError`, `InodeKind`, `FileAttrs`)
599	- Constants: UPPER_SNAKE_CASE (`ROOT_INO`, `BLOCK_SIZE`)
600	
601	### Documentation
602	
603	- `//!` module-level doc comments in `lib.rs` and `mod.rs`
604	- `///` doc comments on all public items
605	- `#[cfg(feature = "...")]` for platform-specific code (e.g., `fuse`, `winfsp`)
606	
607	## Binary Data Handling
608	
609	### TypeScript
610	
611	- **Always use `Uint8Array`** for binary data, never raw `ArrayBuffer`
612	- **Never use `.buffer` on `Uint8Array` for Blob construction** -- it returns the entire underlying ArrayBuffer which may be larger than the view:
613	
614	```typescript
615	// WRONG -- silent data corruption
616	new Blob([uint8array.buffer]);
617	
618	// CORRECT
619	new Blob([uint8array]);
620	```
621	
622	- Hex encoding/decoding via `@cipherbox/crypto` utilities: `hexToBytes()`, `bytesToHex()`
623	- API transport: hex-encoded strings for keys, base64 for encrypted data blobs
624	
625	### Rust
626	
627	- `Vec<u8>` for owned byte data
628	- `&[u8]` for borrowed byte data
629	- `Zeroizing<Vec<u8>>` for sensitive key material
630	- `hex::encode()` / `hex::decode()` for hex conversion
631	
632	## Error Handling Patterns Per Layer
633	
634	### Package Layer (crypto, core, sdk-core)
635	
636	Custom typed errors. Never throw generic `Error`:
637	
638	```typescript
639	throw new CryptoError('Encryption failed', 'ENCRYPTION_FAILED');
640	```
641	
642	### SDK Layer (sdk)
643	
644	`withOperation()` wrapper emits `operation:start`, `operation:end`, and `error` events. Errors propagate to caller:
645	
646	```typescript
647	// SdkEventEmitter catches subscriber errors silently
648	try {
649	  handler(event);
650	} catch {
651	  /* subscriber bugs don't crash SDK */
652	}
653	```
654	
655	### API Backend (NestJS)
656	
657	Throw NestJS HTTP exceptions:
658	
659	```typescript
660	throw new ConflictException('Vault already exists for this user');
661	throw new NotFoundException('Vault not found');
662	throw new BadRequestException('Invalid input');
663	```
664	
665	### Frontend Services
666	
667	Retry wrapper with exponential backoff for network operations:
668	
669	```typescript
670	async function withRetry<T>(fn: () => Promise<T>, maxRetries = 3, baseDelay = 500): Promise<T>;
671	```
672	
673	Error detection via status code inspection:
674	
675	```typescript
676	export function isConflictError(error: unknown): boolean {
677	  const e = error as Record<string, unknown>;
678	  return e.status === 409;
679	}
680	```
681	
682	### Frontend Hooks
683	
684	Combine loading/error state from sub-hooks. Errors clear when next operation starts.
685	
686	### React Async Safety
687	
688	- Re-check refs for null after every `await` in async callbacks
689	- Never use non-null assertions (`!`) on refs in async code
690	- Wrap `HTMLMediaElement.play()` in try/catch (autoplay policy)
691	
692	## API Client Generation Workflow
693	
694	After modifying any `.dto.ts`, `.controller.ts`, or `.entity.ts` in `apps/api`:
695	
696	```bash
697	pnpm api:generate
698	```
699	
700	This command:
701	
702	1. Generates OpenAPI spec from NestJS decorators (`pnpm openapi:generate`)
703	2. Regenerates typed client functions via Orval (`pnpm --filter @cipherbox/api-client generate`)
704	3. Builds the api-client package
705	4. Runs lint fix across the monorepo
706	
707	The pre-commit hook (`scripts/check-api-client.sh`) blocks commits that modify API source files without staging the regenerated `packages/api-client/openapi.json`.
708	
709	## Logging
710	
711	### Backend
712	
713	NestJS default logger (console output). No Winston or structured logging framework currently configured.
714	
715	**Security rules:**
716	
717	- NEVER log `privateKey`, `folderKey`, `fileKey`, or any encryption keys
718	- NEVER log full request/response bodies containing encrypted data
719	
720	### Frontend
721	
722	`console.log` / `console.warn` / `console.error` for development.
723	
724	### Rust Desktop
725	
726	`env_logger` + `log` crate macros:
727	
728	```rust
729	log::info!("CipherBox Desktop starting...");
730	log::error!("Failed to build tray: {}", e);
731	```
732	
733	## Comments and Documentation
734	
735	### TypeScript
736	
737	- JSDoc with `@example` blocks on public API functions and major components
738	- Module-level JSDoc on `index.ts` barrel files (especially in packages)
739	- Inline comments for non-obvious behavior, security considerations marked with `[SECURITY: ...]`
740	- `_` prefix for intentionally unused variables (enforced by ESLint)
741	
742	### Rust
743	
744	- `//!` for module-level documentation
745	- `///` for public item documentation
746	- `// SAFETY:` comments required near `unsafe` blocks
747	- Section dividers with `// -- SectionName ---...` pattern
748	
749	## Security Conventions
750	
751	- **Memory-only keys:** `Uint8Array` key material is never persisted to localStorage/sessionStorage
752	- **Zero-fill on cleanup:** `Uint8Array.fill(0)` before releasing references (stores, SDK `destroy()`)
753	- **Rust:** `Zeroizing<Vec<u8>>` for automatic zeroization on drop
754	- **ECIES wrapping:** All key transport uses ECIES (secp256k1); server never sees plaintext keys
755	- **Generic crypto errors:** Error messages from crypto layer are deliberately vague to prevent oracle attacks
756	- **Exposed dev stores:** Zustand stores attached to `window.__ZUSTAND_*` only when `import.meta.env.DEV`
757	
758	---
759	
760	<!-- Convention analysis: 2026-03-27 -->
761	
```

{
  "file_path": "/Users/michael/Code/cipher-box/.learnings/2026-03-30-test-mock-type-safety.md",
  "content": "# Test Mock Type Safety in NestJS and Vitest\n\n**Date:** 2026-03-30\n\n## Original Prompt\n\n> Fix pre-existing test errors in the SDK and API test suites.\n\n## What I Learned\n\n- **`jest.Mocked<Partial<T>>` loses mock methods after `module.get()`**: When a test declares `let service: jest.Mocked<Partial<SomeService>>` and assigns via `module.get(SomeService)`, the return type is `SomeService` (not mocked). The `.mockResolvedValue()` calls then fail to typecheck because the property is typed as the real method signature, not `jest.Mock`.\n\n- **Vitest `vi.mock()` with bare factory drops non-mocked exports**: When sdk-core tests used `vi.mock('@cipherbox/sdk-core', () => ({ uploadFile: vi.fn(), ... }))`, any export not listed (like `selectEncryptionMode`) was undefined at runtime. The fix is `vi.mock('@cipherbox/sdk-core', async (importOriginal) => { const actual = await importOriginal(); return { ...actual, uploadFile: vi.fn() }; })`.\n\n- **The pattern is systemic**: Found this in 10+ test files across `packages/sdk/` and `apps/api/`. Each file independently made the same mistake. This suggests the pattern was copy-pasted from an early test.\n\n## Correct Patterns\n\n**NestJS spec files (Jest):** Type the mock directly with the shape you need:\n```typescript\nlet mockService: { methodA: jest.Mock; methodB: jest.Mock };\n// assign from module.get() with cast\nmockService = module.get(RealService) as unknown as typeof mockService;\n```\n\n**Vitest module mocks:** Always spread `importOriginal()` so non-mocked exports survive:\n```typescript\nvi.mock('@cipherbox/sdk-core', async (importOriginal) => {\n  const actual = await importOriginal<typeof import('@cipherbox/sdk-core')>();\n  return { ...actual, uploadFile: vi.fn() };\n});\n```\n\n## Key Files\n\n- `packages/sdk/src/__tests__/*.test.ts` -- All SDK test files that mock sdk-core\n- `apps/api/src/republish/*.spec.ts` -- Republish service/processor/health controller specs\n- `apps/api/src/tee/tee.service.spec.ts` -- TEE service spec\n- `apps/api/src/vault/vault.controller.spec.ts` -- Vault controller spec\n"
}
```

  "old_string": "## Security Conventions",
  "new_string": "## Testing Conventions\n\n### Mock Typing in NestJS Specs (Jest)\n\n**Never use `jest.Mocked<Partial<T>>`** for mocks retrieved via `module.get()`. The `module.get()` return type is the real service, not the mock, so `.mockResolvedValue()` fails to typecheck.\n\n```typescript\n// WRONG -- loses mock methods after module.get()\nlet service: jest.Mocked<Partial<MyService>>;\nservice = module.get(MyService); // typed as MyService, not jest.Mock\n\n// CORRECT -- type the mock shape directly\nlet mockService: { myMethod: jest.Mock; otherMethod: jest.Mock };\n// ...\nmockService = module.get(MyService) as unknown as typeof mockService;\n```\n\nAlternatively, keep a reference to the mock object created in `beforeEach` and use it directly:\n\n```typescript\nlet mockService: { myMethod: jest.Mock };\n\nbeforeEach(async () => {\n  mockService = { myMethod: jest.fn() };\n  const module = await Test.createTestingModule({\n    providers: [{ provide: MyService, useValue: mockService }],\n  }).compile();\n  // No need to module.get() -- use mockService directly\n});\n```\n\n### Module Mocking in Vitest\n\n**Always use `importOriginal` when partially mocking a module.** Bare factory mocks replace the entire module, dropping any export you don't explicitly list:\n\n```typescript\n// WRONG -- drops all non-listed exports (e.g., selectEncryptionMode)\nvi.mock('@cipherbox/sdk-core', () => ({\n  uploadFile: vi.fn(),\n  downloadAndDecrypt: vi.fn(),\n}));\n\n// CORRECT -- real exports survive alongside mocked functions\nvi.mock('@cipherbox/sdk-core', async (importOriginal) => {\n  const actual = await importOriginal<typeof import('@cipherbox/sdk-core')>();\n  return {\n    ...actual,\n    uploadFile: vi.fn(),\n    downloadAndDecrypt: vi.fn(),\n  };\n});\n```\n\n**When to use bare factory:** Only when you want to replace every export in the module (rare).\n\n### Test Entity Mocks\n\nWhen mocking TypeORM entities, include **all required fields** from the entity class. Missing fields cause TS errors that accumulate silently. When a migration adds a column to an entity, grep for that entity's test mocks and update them.\n\n## Security Conventions"
}
```

1	# CipherBox Memory
2	
3	## CRITICAL: Branch Protection & Signing Trust
4	- **NEVER commit directly to `main`** — no exceptions, not even docs/todo moves
5	- Commit signing requirement **disabled** on branch protection (2026-02-13) — GSD agent subprocesses can't access 1Password SSH agent
6	- Tag protection still requires signatures; GitHub signs merge/release commits automatically
7	- Git config `commit.gpgsign=true` still set — signing happens when 1Password agent is available
8	- **Do NOT intentionally bypass signing** — no `-c commit.gpgsign=false`, no `--no-gpg-sign`
9	- Unsigned commits from GSD agents are acceptable since PRs are squash-merged by GitHub with its own signature
10	- **First action when starting ANY task:** determine the feature branch name, then switch to it
11	  - If on `main`: `git checkout -b feat/<name>` immediately, before ANY other work
12	  - If on a feature branch: verify it's the RIGHT branch for this task
13	  - If on the WRONG feature branch: `git checkout main && git pull && git checkout -b feat/<new-name>`
14	- Always branch off from latest `main` (`git pull` first)
15	- **Before pushing follow-up commits to a branch**, check if the PR was already merged: `gh pr list --head <branch> --state merged`
16	  - CI workflow fixes often get merged quickly, so always verify before pushing additional commits
17	  - If merged: `git checkout main && git pull && git checkout -b fix/<new-name>` and cherry-pick or redo the change
18	- The todo-move step in /gsd:check-todos must also happen on the feature branch, not main
19	- This rule applies to ALL commit types: code, docs, config, todo moves, state updates — everything
20	
21	## SDK Testing & Load Testing Strategy
22	- [project_testing_strategy.md](project_testing_strategy.md) — SDK E2E / load test architecture, rate limiting bypass design, horizontal scaling test strategy, setApiClientConfig singleton caveat
23	- In-repo doc: `tests/TESTING_STRATEGY.md`
24	
25	## Dev Environment
26	- Docker is NOT needed to start services - all Docker dependencies (PostgreSQL, IPFS, Redis) run on the host machine at `192.168.133.12`
27	- API `.env` points DB/IPFS/Redis to `192.168.133.12` (ports: PG 5432, IPFS 5001/8080, Redis 6380)
28	- Just start the API (`pnpm --filter api dev`) and frontend (`pnpm --filter web dev`)
29	- Frontend: http://localhost:5173
30	- API: http://localhost:3000
31	- For SDK E2E tests: set `NODE_ENV=test` in `apps/api/.env` (relaxes throttle limits), start mock-ipns-routing, then run tests
32	
33	## Playwright Login
34	- E2E credentials in `tests/web-e2e/.env`: email `test_account_4718@example.com`, OTP `851527`
35	- Flow: Click [CONNECT] -> Continue with Email/Phone -> enter email -> submit -> enter OTP digits -> wait ~10s for auth
36	
37	## Known Bugs (discovered Phase 7.1 UAT)
38	- **Upload modal stuck:** FIXED in PR #56
39	- **Auth refresh race:** FIXED in PR #58
40	- **IPNS resolve 502:** delegated-ipfs.dev unreliable. Fix: fallback to DB-cached CID
41	
42	## Parallel Agent Work
43	- **Use git worktrees for parallel agents**, not branch switching on a shared working tree
44	- Always commit lockfile changes when adding dev dependencies (CI uses `--frozen-lockfile`)
45	- [feedback_worktrees.md](feedback_worktrees.md) — NEVER remove/reuse existing worktrees; always create new ones with unique names
46	
47	## Phase 9 Desktop Client — UAT Progress
48	- **Branch:** `feat/phase-9-desktop-client`
49	- **Tests 1-14 passed**, Test 2 has open issue (tray status flaky), Test 15 pending
50	- **Currently testing:** Test 15 (Quit unmounts cleanly)
51	- **16 fixes applied** (9 from previous session + 7 this session)
52	- **Before merge:** remove `eprintln!(">>> ` lines, clean stale mount on startup
53	
54	## FUSE-T Key Lessons
55	- **SWITCHED TO SMB BACKEND**: NFS backend has unfixable macOS kernel bug — WRITE RPCs never reach FUSE-T for new files. SMB backend works correctly. Mount option: `backend=smb`.
56	- **Vendored fuser with socket-read patch**: FUSE-T communicates via Unix domain socket, not /dev/fuse. Stock fuser does single read() expecting atomic messages, but sockets fragment large messages and coalesce small ones. Fix: peek at 4-byte header length, then loop-read exactly that many bytes. Patch in `vendor/fuser/src/channel.rs`.
57	- **SMB opendir must return non-zero fh**: SMB backend treats `fh=0` as invalid handle. Use `next_fh.fetch_add(1)`.
58	- **SMB rename FIXED**: Was failing with EPERM because `access(W_OK)` checked `req.uid() != attr.uid` — under SMB, UIDs differ. Fix: `access()` always grants (encryption is access control). UID in `create()`/`mkdir()` unified to `getuid()`.
59	- **FUSE-T truncates filenames in rename callback** by 8 bytes — need suffix-match fallback
60	- **NFS single-thread**: ALL callbacks on one thread. ANY blocking call stalls everything.
61	- **NEVER block in read()**: Even 500ms blocks cause Finder "connection lost". Use channel-based prefetch.
62	- **readdir/lookup consistency is critical**: NFS client does LOOKUP for every readdir entry including ".." — ENOENT causes disconnect
63	- **NFS inode stability is critical**: populate_folder MUST reuse existing inode numbers. Allocating new inos for same-name children causes NFS "stale file handle" disconnects.
64	- **Background refresh race**: async metadata refresh can overwrite local mutations. Use mutation cooldown (30s).
65	- **Deduplicate readdir refreshes**: NFS calls readdir at offset=0 and offset=N — only fire refresh on offset=0.
66	- **macOS keychain**: `keyring::set_password()` fails if item exists — always delete first. Debug builds skip Keychain entirely (each rebuild = new binary signature = repeated prompts).
67	- **Stale mount cleanup**: After crash, ~/CipherBox may contain .DS_Store. App should rm contents before mount.
68	- **Force unmount**: `umount` fails when Finder has handles open. Use `diskutil unmount force` as fallback.
69	- **FUSE-T logs**: Check `~/Library/Logs/fuse-t/fuse-t.log` for NFS/SMB-level errors.
70	
71	## Tauri Webview Lessons
72	- **Web3Auth clearCache() vs logout({cleanup:true})**: cleanup:true tears down connectors, causing "Wallet connector not ready". Use clearCache() to clear session without destroying SDK state.
73	- **Window reuse**: Existing window created with on_new_window handler keeps it across show/hide. Don't destroy+recreate (label conflict). Just reload page with eval("location.reload()").
74	- **OAuth popup cleanup**: Destroy oauth-popup-* windows after auth completes in Rust handle_auth_complete.
75	
76	## Staging Environment
77	- **Branch:** `feat/phase-9.1`
78	- **VPS:** 76.13.151.200 (Hostinger), SSH key `~/.ssh/ci-deploy-key`
79	- **API:** https://api-staging.cipherbox.cc (Caddy reverse proxy)
80	- **Web:** https://app-staging.cipherbox.cc (Caddy static files)
81	- **Domains:** use flat subdomains (`api-staging`, not `api.staging`) — Cloudflare Universal SSL only covers `*.cipherbox.cc`
82	- **Deploy:** push tag `staging-v<version>-rc-<N>` (e.g., `staging-v0.26.0-rc-1`) triggers `.github/workflows/deploy-staging.yml`. Previous `v*-staging*` format caused release-please to match staging pre-releases to root package path, blocking tag creation.
83	- **GitHub Environment:** `staging` — secrets/vars split (vars for non-sensitive, secrets for sensitive)
84	- **Docker Compose `.env`:** must be named `.env` (not `.env.staging`) for variable substitution
85	- **pnpm Docker:** use `pnpm deploy --prod --legacy` for flat node_modules without symlinks
86	- **Cloudflare IPFS gateway deprecated** — serve web app from Caddy instead
87	- See `.learnings/2026-02-09-staging-deployment-first-deploy.md` for full details
88	
89	## Pencil MCP Lessons
90	- **Changes are in-memory only** — `batch_design` does NOT write to `.pen` file on disk. User must save from Pencil editor. Ask user to save periodically during long sessions to avoid data loss if MCP disconnects.
91	- **Modal overlay pattern**: Copy base screen, switch container to `layout: "none"`, set x/y/width/height on children explicitly, layer backdrop + modal on top.
92	- **Copy gives new IDs**: After `C()`, must `batch_get` to discover new descendant IDs. Never `U()` descendants of a just-copied node in the same batch.
93	- **Multi-screen workflow**: Create all placeholder frames first (`placeholder: true`), then build content, then remove placeholders when done.
94	- See `.learnings/2026-02-09-pencil-mcp-multi-screen-design.md` for full details.
95	
96	## CRITICAL: TypeORM Migration Discipline
97	- **`synchronize: true` in dev/test hides missing migrations** — tables auto-created from entities, so missing `CREATE TABLE` migrations are invisible until staging/production deploy
98	- **Every new `@Entity()` MUST have a corresponding `CREATE TABLE` migration** — check `apps/api/src/migrations/` before merging any PR that adds entities
99	- **FullSchema baseline does NOT need updating** — it's a point-in-time snapshot; incremental migrations with `IF NOT EXISTS` run on both fresh and existing databases
100	- **Use `IF NOT EXISTS`** for idempotency (pattern: `1740000000000-AddDeviceApprovals.ts`)
101	- **Migration timestamp ordering matters** — create-table migration must have earlier timestamp than any migration that modifies that table
102	- Phase 14 hit this exact bug: `SharesPartialUniqueIndex` tried to modify `shares` table that didn't exist on staging. Fixed in PR #186.
103	- **Full protocol:** `docs/DATABASE_EVOLUTION_PROTOCOL.md`
104	
105	## CRITICAL: Run E2E Tests Before Pushing
106	- **Always run the relevant E2E test suite before pushing** — CI feedback takes 15-20 min, local/staging runs are immediate
107	- **Run against staging** (most reliable): `BASE_URL=https://app-staging.cipherbox.cc pnpm --filter @cipherbox/web-e2e exec playwright test tests/<feature>.spec.ts --timeout 180000`
108	- Playwright's `webServer` auto-start often doesn't work locally; start API + web manually, or just use staging
109	- **NEVER commit E2E fixes blind and hope CI passes** — always repro locally first
110	- See `.learnings/2026-03-29-e2e-test-debugging-workflow.md` and `.learnings/2026-02-24-run-e2e-locally-before-push.md`
111	
112	## Conventional Commits & Branch Naming
113	- **Choose the commit type carefully** — it flows into changelogs and release notes via Release Please
114	- `fix:` implies a **bug fix** and triggers a **patch version bump** — do not use for config cleanup or maintenance
115	- `chore:` is correct for non-functional changes (config, tooling, dependency cleanup, removing unused settings)
116	- `feat:` triggers a **minor version bump** — only use for actual new features
117	- Branch prefix should match the commit type: `chore/remove-statusline-config`, not `fix/...`
118	- Misusing `fix:` for non-bugfix changes pollutes the changelog with fake "Bug Fixes" entries
119	- When in doubt, prefer `chore:` over `fix:` — a missing changelog entry is less harmful than a misleading one
120	
121	## Patterns & Lessons
122	- Always verify state machines end-to-end in UAT, not just individual transitions
123	- For async deduplication, share the Promise, not a boolean flag
124	- Never use eventually-consistent external services as sole source of truth
125	- Playwright UAT catches UX issues that code review and static verification miss
126	- FUSE-T NFS is much more fragile than kernel FUSE — every inconsistency breaks the mount
127	- **Zustand stale closures in async callbacks**: React hook selectors (`useFolderStore()`) capture state at render time. Inside `useCallback` async functions, use `useFolderStore.getState()` to read fresh state — especially when effects in other hooks modify the store in the same render commit.
128	- **`useInterval` doesn't fire immediately**: Need a separate `useEffect` for fire-on-mount behavior.
129	- **GitHub PR review comments workflow**: See `.learnings/2026-02-10-pr-review-comment-workflow.md` for full details. Key points:
130	  - Use GraphQL `reviewThreads` query (not REST) to get `isResolved` status per thread
131	  - Reply via REST: `gh api repos/OWNER/REPO/pulls/N/comments --field in_reply_to=COMMENT_ID --raw-field body='...'` (use `--field` not `-f` for `in_reply_to` — it must be sent as integer, `-f` sends string which causes 422)
132	  - Resolve via GraphQL: `resolveReviewThread(input: {threadId: "..."})` — can batch multiple in one mutation
133	  - Always check code state before acting — comments may already be addressed in later commits
134	  - For already-addressed comments: reply with explanation of where/how it was fixed, then resolve
135	  - For inapplicable comments: reply with reasoning for not making changes, then resolve
136	- **Don't justify code duplication with build optimization**: "Vite dead-code eliminates the unused branch" doesn't matter — maintainability and avoiding divergent code paths matters more.
137	- **GSD UI keyword detection must be kept in sync** across 4 locations: `quick.md:105`, `discuss-phase.md:505`, `design/sync.md` (ui_keyword_heuristic section), `execute-phase.md` workflow (design_sync_check step). The old `gsd-verifier.md:699` location no longer exists. When adding new UI-related keywords, update all four. The list was expanded in Feb 2026 to include interaction keywords (drag, drop, hover, animation, etc.) after DnD task wasn't detected as UI work.
138	
139	## GSD Workflow Branching
140	- [feedback_gsd_branching.md](feedback_gsd_branching.md) — All GSD steps for a phase stay on ONE branch; never create new branches mid-workflow
141	- In-repo learnings: `.learnings/2026-03-24-gsd-ui-phase-branching.md`
142	
143	## API Console OTP
144	- [feedback_api_console_otp.md](feedback_api_console_otp.md) — pnpm dev output is piped to task output files on disk; always grep those files for OTPs instead of overcomplicating with DB queries or auth bypasses
145	
146	## E2E Test Debugging
147	- [feedback_e2e_debugging.md](feedback_e2e_debugging.md) — NEVER push E2E fixes blind; always repro against staging first
148	
149	## Metadata Schema Documentation
150	- **Schema reference:** `docs/METADATA_SCHEMAS.md` -- documents all 10 metadata objects (FolderMetadata, FileMetadata, DeviceRegistry, etc.) with field tables, encryption, storage, source file cross-references
151	- **Evolution protocol:** `docs/METADATA_EVOLUTION_PROTOCOL.md` -- formal rules for evolving metadata schemas (additive vs breaking changes, version bump rules, dual-platform checklist)
152	- **ALWAYS consult these docs** before any ticket that adds/removes/changes fields on metadata objects
153	- Evolution checklist (Section 4 of protocol) must be completed for every schema change
154	- Key rule: optional fields with sensible defaults = no version bump; removing/changing fields = version bump required
155	
```

  "content": "---\nname: Fix test type errors proactively\ndescription: Always fix test type errors when noticed during typecheck — don't leave them as pre-existing\ntype: feedback\n---\n\nWhen running `tsc --noEmit` on any package, fix ALL type errors in test files — not just errors in files you changed. Pre-existing test errors accumulate silently and mask real regressions.\n\n**Why:** The user had 10+ test files with stale mock types across `apps/api/` and `packages/sdk/`. These were invisible during `pnpm test` (Jest/Vitest don't typecheck) but failed `tsc --noEmit`. Each one was a 30-second fix that had been deferred for weeks.\n\n**How to apply:**\n- After any `tsc --noEmit`, if there are errors in `.spec.ts` or `.test.ts` files, fix them in the same PR\n- Common patterns to watch for: `jest.Mocked<Partial<T>>` losing mock methods after `module.get()`, missing entity fields added by migrations, bare `vi.mock()` factories dropping non-mocked exports\n- Conventions documented in `.planning/codebase/CONVENTIONS.md` (Testing Conventions section)\n- Learnings captured in `.learnings/2026-03-30-test-mock-type-safety.md`\n"
}

Done. Here's a summary of everything in this branch:

**Bug fixes:**
- **`PATCH /vault/byo-status`** — New endpoint to toggle BYO mode. `setByoStatus()` was a dead service method with no controller. This was why `register-cid` always returned 403 (not 400 as originally thought — the real first error is the BYO check)
- **Load test `ipns-publish`** — Was sending `{ ipnsName, cid }` but `PublishIpnsEntryDto` requires `{ ipnsName, record, metadataCid }`. Replaced with `createAndPublishIpnsRecord` from sdk-core which creates a proper signed record
- **`createByoClientPool`** — Now enables BYO mode on test accounts after creation via the new endpoint
- **9 API test files** — Fixed `jest.Mocked<Partial<T>>` pattern, missing entity fields, and `RequestWithUser` casts

**Documentation:**
- `.learnings/2026-03-30-test-mock-type-safety.md` — Captures the systemic mock typing issue
- `.planning/codebase/CONVENTIONS.md` — New Testing Conventions section with correct patterns
- Memory updated to proactively fix test errors going forward

5 unresolved threads. Let me triage:

1. **createSdkContext per iteration** — Valid, easy fix
2. **Response DTO mismatch** — Valid, should add a proper DTO
3. **Missing controller tests** — Already addressed in 370fe734c
4. **BYO enable failure silently swallowed** — Valid, should filter out failed clients
5. **TOCTOU in setByoStatus** — Not applicable — the vault can't be deleted while the user is authenticated (no delete vault endpoint), and even if it could, `setByoStatus` does an UPDATE with WHERE clause that simply affects 0 rows

Let me implement fixes for 1, 2, and 4.

{
  "replace_all": false,
  "file_path": "/Users/michael/Code/cipher-box/tests/load/src/harness/client-pool.ts",
  "old_string": "  // Enable BYO mode on each account's vault so register-cid is authorized\n  await Promise.all(\n    byoPool.map(async (pc) => {\n      try {\n        const ctx = createSdkContext(pc);\n        await ctx.axiosInstance!.patch('/vault/byo-status', { isByo: true });\n      } catch (err) {\n        console.warn(`[Client ${pc.id}] Failed to enable BYO mode: ${(err as Error).message}`);\n      }\n    })\n  );",
  "new_string": "  // Enable BYO mode on each account's vault so register-cid is authorized\n  const byoResults = await Promise.allSettled(\n    byoPool.map(async (pc) => {\n      const ctx = createSdkContext(pc);\n      await ctx.axiosInstance!.patch('/vault/byo-status', { isByo: true });\n      return pc;\n    })\n  );\n\n  const enabledPool: ByoPoolClient[] = [];\n  for (const result of byoResults) {\n    if (result.status === 'fulfilled') {\n      enabledPool.push(result.value);\n    } else {\n      console.warn(`BYO enable failed for a client: ${result.reason}`);\n    }\n  }\n\n  if (enabledPool.length === 0) {\n    throw new Error('Failed to enable BYO mode on any client — cannot run BYO scenarios');\n  }\n  if (enabledPool.length < byoPool.length) {\n    console.warn(\n      `BYO mode enabled on ${enabledPool.length}/${byoPool.length} clients — continuing with partial pool`\n    );\n  }"
}

> AGENT

To answer your question: `.git/info/exclude` only prevents `git add` from picking up **untracked** files. The `commit-msg.pre-entire` file wasn't showing in `git status` or `git diff` — it was untracked and ignored. The files that *were* showing in `git status --short` were `.gitignore` (modified, staged), `.husky/commit-msg` (modified, staged), and `.learnings/...` (new, staged). The `pre-entire` file only came up when I tried to `git add` it explicitly.

  "content": "---\ncreated: 2026-03-30T00:14:11.681Z\ntitle: Exclude Rust target/ directory from ESLint\narea: tooling\nfiles:\n  - eslint.config.js\n  - apps/api/src/ipns/delegated-routing.client.spec.ts:60\n---\n\n## Problem\n\n`pnpm lint:fix` (run as final step of `pnpm api:generate`) reports 262 problems, but 261 are false positives from Rust build artifacts in `target/`:\n\n- `target/debug/build/cipherbox-desktop-*/out/__global-api-script.js` — Tauri codegen (4 errors)\n- `target/doc/static.files/*.js` — rustdoc output (~150 errors: `no-undef` for rustdoc globals)\n- `target/doc/search.index/*.js` — rustdoc search index (~20 errors)\n- `target/doc/src-files.js`, `target/doc/search.index/**` — more rustdoc output\n\nThese are generated files that should never be linted. The `target/` directory is already in `.gitignore` but not in ESLint's ignore list.\n\nThe remaining 1 legitimate warning is `@typescript-eslint/no-explicit-any` in `delegated-routing.client.spec.ts:60`.\n\n## Solution\n\n1. Add `target/` to the `ignores` array in `eslint.config.js`\n2. Optionally fix the single `no-explicit-any` warning in `delegated-routing.client.spec.ts:60`\n3. 5 threads. Let me triage:

1. **register-cid error swallowing** — Valid, now that BYO is enabled upfront, 403 is unexpected. Should log non-403 errors.
2. **ipns-publish metric comment inaccuracy** — Valid, easy comment fix.
3. **openapi.json missing securitySchemes** — Not applicable — this is how NestJS/Swagger generates the spec; orval handles it fine. The spec works in production.
4. **MigrationStatusDto.completedAt as object** — Not applicable — this is a pre-existing issue in the TEE migration DTO from Phase 35, not introduced by this PR.
5. **Same as #4** — generated type mirrors the schema, same root cause.

Consider enabling BYO in bounded-size batches (or with a concurrency limiter) and logging which"

> AGENT

Triaging:

1. **BYO enable batching for large pools** — Valid point but low priority. The capacity ceiling scenarios run infrequently and the PATCH endpoint is lightweight. Not worth adding batching complexity now.
2. **`any` in SpyInstance cast** — Already addressed by the background agent earlier. The `any` is in the pre-existing `delaySpy` declaration (line 37), suppressed by the existing eslint-disable comment. The cast on line 61 just matches that type.
3. **findVault overhead in setByoStatus** — Valid optimization but premature. `findVault` is a single DB query + one TEE key query. The BYO toggle is called once per account, not in a hot path.

All three are not worth changing — let me reply and resolve.

1	name: Release Gate
2	
3	on:
4	  pull_request:
5	    branches: [main]
6	    types: [opened, synchronize, reopened]
7	
8	jobs:
9	  detect-changes:
10	    name: Detect Changes Since Last Release
11	    runs-on: ubuntu-latest
12	    outputs:
13	      desktop: ${{ steps.check.outputs.desktop || steps.release-check.outputs.desktop }}
14	      prev_tag: ${{ steps.check.outputs.prev_tag || steps.release-check.outputs.prev_tag }}
15	      is_release: ${{ steps.release-check.outputs.is_release }}
16	    steps:
17	      - name: Check if release PR
18	        id: release-check
19	        env:
20	          HEAD_REF: ${{ github.head_ref }}
21	        run: |
22	          if [[ "$HEAD_REF" == release-please--* ]]; then
23	            echo "is_release=true" >> "$GITHUB_OUTPUT"
24	          else
25	            echo "is_release=false" >> "$GITHUB_OUTPUT"
26	            echo "desktop=false" >> "$GITHUB_OUTPUT"
27	            echo "prev_tag=" >> "$GITHUB_OUTPUT"
28	            echo "Not a release PR — skipping change detection"
29	          fi
30	
31	      - uses: actions/checkout@v4
32	        if: steps.release-check.outputs.is_release == 'true'
33	        with:
34	          fetch-depth: 0
35	
36	      - name: Check for desktop-related changes since last release
37	        if: steps.release-check.outputs.is_release == 'true'
38	        id: check
39	        run: |
40	          # Find the latest non-staging release tag (cipher-box-v* format)
41	          PREV_TAG=$(git tag --list 'cipher-box-v*' --sort=-v:refname \
42	            | grep -v 'staging' \
43	            | head -1)
44	          # Fallback to legacy v* tags if no cipher-box-v* tags exist
45	          if [ -z "$PREV_TAG" ]; then
46	            PREV_TAG=$(git tag --list 'v*' --sort=-v:refname \
47	              | grep -v 'staging' \
48	              | grep -v '@' \
49	              | head -1)
50	          fi
51	
52	          if [ -z "$PREV_TAG" ]; then
53	            echo "No previous release tag found — assuming desktop changed"
54	            echo "desktop=true" >> "$GITHUB_OUTPUT"
55	            echo "prev_tag=" >> "$GITHUB_OUTPUT"
56	            exit 0
57	          fi
58	
59	          echo "Previous release tag: ${PREV_TAG}"
60	          echo "prev_tag=${PREV_TAG}" >> "$GITHUB_OUTPUT"
61	          # Use origin/main (not HEAD) so that gh-run-rerun'd workflows
62	          # compare against the current main, not a stale GITHUB_SHA.
63	          git fetch origin main
64	          MAIN_REF="origin/main"
65	          echo "Diffing ${PREV_TAG}..${MAIN_REF}"
66	
67	          # Desktop-related path patterns (mirrored from ci-e2e.yml)
68	          DESKTOP_PATTERNS=(
69	            'apps/desktop/src/'
70	            'apps/desktop/src-tauri/src/'
71	            'apps/desktop/src-tauri/vendor/'
72	            'apps/desktop/src-tauri/capabilities/'
73	            'apps/desktop/src-tauri/resources/'
74	            'apps/desktop/src-tauri/Cargo.toml'
75	            'apps/desktop/src-tauri/build.rs'
76	            'apps/desktop/src-tauri/rust-toolchain.toml'
77	            'apps/desktop/index.html'
78	            'apps/desktop/vite.config.'
79	            'apps/desktop/tsconfig'
80	            'crates/'
81	            'Cargo.toml'
82	            'Cargo.lock'
83	            'tests/vectors/'
84	            'packages/crypto/src/'
85	            'packages/crypto/tsconfig'
86	            '.github/workflows/desktop-e2e.yml'
87	            '.github/workflows/ci-e2e.yml'
88	          )
89	
90	          CHANGED_FILES=$(git diff --name-only "${PREV_TAG}..${MAIN_REF}")
91	          DESKTOP_CHANGED=false
92	
93	          for pattern in "${DESKTOP_PATTERNS[@]}"; do
94	            if echo "$CHANGED_FILES" | grep -Fq "$pattern"; then
95	              echo "Desktop change detected matching pattern: ${pattern}"
96	              DESKTOP_CHANGED=true
97	              break
98	            fi
99	          done
100	
101	          echo "desktop=${DESKTOP_CHANGED}" >> "$GITHUB_OUTPUT"
102	          echo "Desktop changes detected: ${DESKTOP_CHANGED}"
103	
104	  verify-e2e:
105	    name: Verify E2E Passed
106	    needs: detect-changes
107	    if: ${{ always() && !cancelled() }}
108	    runs-on: ubuntu-latest
109	    timeout-minutes: 40
110	    steps:
111	      - name: Fail if detect-changes did not complete (release PR)
112	        if: startsWith(github.head_ref, 'release-please--') && needs.detect-changes.result != 'success'
113	        run: |
114	          echo "::error::detect-changes must succeed before E2E verification."
115	          exit 1
116	
117	      - name: Skip (not a release PR)
118	        if: "!startsWith(github.head_ref, 'release-please--')"
119	        run: echo "Not a release PR — E2E gate not applicable"
120	
121	      - name: Wait for Web E2E and verify it passed
122	        if: startsWith(github.head_ref, 'release-please--')
123	        env:
124	          GH_TOKEN: ${{ secrets.GITHUB_TOKEN }}
125	        run: |
126	          # Get the latest commit on main (the base the release PR targets)
127	          MAIN_SHA=$(gh api repos/${{ github.repository }}/git/ref/heads/main --jq '.object.sha')
128	          echo "Latest main commit: ${MAIN_SHA}"
129	
130	          # Poll for the CI E2E orchestrator run to complete (it triggers on the same push)
131	          # 40 * 30s = 20 min — matches the web-e2e.yml job timeout
132	          MAX_ATTEMPTS=40
133	          POLL_INTERVAL=30
134	          echo "Waiting for CI E2E run to complete (polling every ${POLL_INTERVAL}s, max ${MAX_ATTEMPTS} attempts)..."
135	
136	          for attempt in $(seq 1 $MAX_ATTEMPTS); do
137	            E2E_CONCLUSION=$(gh run list --repo ${{ github.repository }} \
138	              --workflow=ci-e2e.yml --branch=main --limit=10 \
139	              --json headSha,conclusion,status,databaseId \
140	              --jq "[.[] | select(.headSha == \"${MAIN_SHA}\")][0].conclusion // empty")
141	
142	            if [ -n "$E2E_CONCLUSION" ]; then
143	              echo "CI E2E completed with conclusion: ${E2E_CONCLUSION}"
144	
145	              # Grab the run ID for job-level checks below
146	              E2E_RUN_ID=$(gh run list --repo ${{ github.repository }} \
147	                --workflow=ci-e2e.yml --branch=main --limit=10 \
148	                --json headSha,databaseId \
149	                --jq "[.[] | select(.headSha == \"${MAIN_SHA}\")][0].databaseId")
150	              break
151	            fi
152	
153	            if [ "$attempt" -eq "$MAX_ATTEMPTS" ]; then
154	              echo "::error::CI E2E run did not complete within $(( MAX_ATTEMPTS * POLL_INTERVAL / 60 )) minutes for main (${MAIN_SHA})."
155	              exit 1
156	            fi
157	
158	            echo "Attempt ${attempt}/${MAX_ATTEMPTS}: CI E2E still running, waiting ${POLL_INTERVAL}s..."
159	            sleep $POLL_INTERVAL
160	          done
161	
162	          if [ "$E2E_CONCLUSION" != "success" ]; then
163	            echo "::error::CI E2E orchestrator did not pass on main (conclusion: ${E2E_CONCLUSION})"
164	            exit 1
165	          fi
166	
167	          # Verify the Web E2E job actually ran (not skipped due to no web changes).
168	          # When ci-e2e.yml calls web-e2e.yml via workflow_call, the job name is
169	          # composed as "Web E2E / Web E2E Tests" — contains("Web E2E") matches both.
170	          if ! WEB_E2E_RAN=$(gh api "repos/${{ github.repository }}/actions/runs/${E2E_RUN_ID}/jobs" \
171	            --jq '[.jobs[] | select(.name | contains("Web E2E")) | select(.conclusion == "success")] | length'); then
172	            echo "::error::Failed to query jobs for CI E2E run ${E2E_RUN_ID} (API error)"
173	            exit 1
174	          fi
175	
176	          if [ "${WEB_E2E_RAN:-0}" -eq 0 ]; then
177	            echo "CI E2E passed but Web E2E was skipped (no web changes in latest push)."
178	            echo "Searching for most recent CI E2E run where Web E2E actually executed..."
179	
180	            RECENT_RUN_ID=$(gh run list --repo ${{ github.repository }} \
181	              --workflow=ci-e2e.yml --branch=main --limit=20 \
182	              --json databaseId,conclusion,status \
183	              --jq '[.[] | select(.status == "completed" and .conclusion == "success")] | .[].databaseId' \
184	              | while read -r rid; do
185	                  if ! WEB_COUNT=$(gh api "repos/${{ github.repository }}/actions/runs/${rid}/jobs" \
186	                    --jq '[.jobs[] | select(.name | contains("Web E2E")) | select(.conclusion == "success")] | length'); then
187	                    echo "::warning::Failed to query jobs for run ${rid}, skipping" >&2
188	                    continue
189	                  fi
190	                  if [ "${WEB_COUNT:-0}" -gt 0 ]; then
191	                    echo "$rid"
192	                    break
193	                  fi
194	                done)
195	
196	            if [ -z "$RECENT_RUN_ID" ]; then
197	              echo "::error::No recent CI E2E run found where Web E2E tests actually executed."
198	              exit 1
199	            fi
200	            echo "Web E2E: passed (in CI E2E run ${RECENT_RUN_ID})"
201	          else
202	            echo "Web E2E: passed (at HEAD)"
203	          fi
204	
205	      - name: Verify Desktop E2E (if desktop changed)
206	        if: startsWith(github.head_ref, 'release-please--') && needs.detect-changes.outputs.desktop == 'true'
207	        env:
208	          GH_TOKEN: ${{ secrets.GITHUB_TOKEN }}
209	          PREV_TAG: ${{ needs.detect-changes.outputs.prev_tag }}
210	        run: |
211	          MAIN_SHA=$(gh api repos/${{ github.repository }}/git/ref/heads/main --jq '.object.sha')
212	          echo "Desktop changes detected since ${PREV_TAG} — verifying Desktop E2E"
213	
214	          # Helper: check if a workflow run actually executed desktop-e2e jobs
215	          # (not skipped due to change detection). Returns 0 if tests ran, 1 if skipped, 2 on API error.
216	          run_executed_tests() {
217	            local run_id=$1
218	            local executed
219	            if ! executed=$(gh api "repos/${{ github.repository }}/actions/runs/${run_id}/jobs" \
220	              --jq '[.jobs[] | select(.name | startswith("Desktop E2E")) | select(.conclusion == "success")] | length'); then
221	              echo "::error::Failed to query jobs for run ${run_id} (API error or rate limit)"
222	              return 2
223	            fi
224	            [ "${executed:-0}" -gt 0 ]
225	          }
226	
227	          # First check if there's a desktop E2E run for HEAD (may still be running)
228	          HEAD_RUN=$(gh run list --repo ${{ github.repository }} \
229	            --workflow=ci-e2e.yml --branch=main --limit=50 \
230	            --json headSha,conclusion,status,databaseId \
231	            --jq "[.[] | select(.headSha == \"${MAIN_SHA}\")][0]")
232	
233	          HEAD_STATUS=$(echo "$HEAD_RUN" | jq -r '.status // empty')
234	
235	          if [ -n "$HEAD_STATUS" ]; then
236	            HEAD_RUN_ID=$(echo "$HEAD_RUN" | jq -r '.databaseId')
237	            echo "Desktop E2E run exists for HEAD (run ${HEAD_RUN_ID}) — polling until complete..."
238	            MAX_ATTEMPTS=40
239	            POLL_INTERVAL=30
240	
241	            for attempt in $(seq 1 $MAX_ATTEMPTS); do
242	              CONCLUSION=$(gh run view "$HEAD_RUN_ID" --repo ${{ github.repository }} \
243	                --json conclusion \
244	                --jq '.conclusion // empty')
245	
246	              if [ -n "$CONCLUSION" ]; then
247	                if [ "$CONCLUSION" != "success" ]; then
248	                  echo "::error::Desktop E2E Tests did not pass on main (conclusion: ${CONCLUSION})"
249	                  exit 1
250	                fi
251	                # Verify the desktop-e2e jobs actually ran (not skipped)
252	                run_executed_tests "$HEAD_RUN_ID" && rc=0 || rc=$?
253	                if [ "$rc" -eq 2 ]; then
254	                  exit 1
255	                elif [ "$rc" -eq 0 ]; then
256	                  echo "Desktop E2E: passed (at HEAD, tests executed)"
257	                  exit 0
258	                else
259	                  echo "Desktop E2E run at HEAD succeeded but tests were skipped (no desktop changes detected by workflow)."
260	                  echo "Falling through to search for a run that actually executed tests..."
261	                  break
262	                fi
263	              fi
264	
265	              if [ "$attempt" -eq "$MAX_ATTEMPTS" ]; then
266	                echo "::error::Desktop E2E run did not complete within $(( MAX_ATTEMPTS * POLL_INTERVAL / 60 )) minutes."
267	                exit 1
268	              fi
269	
270	              echo "Attempt ${attempt}/${MAX_ATTEMPTS}: Desktop E2E still running, waiting ${POLL_INTERVAL}s..."
271	              sleep $POLL_INTERVAL
272	            done
273	          fi
274	
275	          # No desktop E2E run for HEAD (or HEAD run skipped tests) — find the
276	          # most recent run in the release range that actually executed tests
277	          echo "Searching for a Desktop E2E run that executed tests since ${PREV_TAG}..."
278	
279	          if [ -z "$PREV_TAG" ]; then
280	            echo "::error::No previous release tag and no Desktop E2E run for HEAD. Cannot verify desktop changes."
281	            exit 1
282	          fi
283	
284	          # Get commit SHAs in the release range
285	          RANGE_COMMITS=$(gh api "repos/${{ github.repository }}/compare/${PREV_TAG}...${MAIN_SHA}" \
286	            --jq '[.commits[].sha] | join("\n")' 2>/dev/null || echo "")
287	
288	          if [ -z "$RANGE_COMMITS" ]; then
289	            echo "::error::Could not determine commits in range ${PREV_TAG}..${MAIN_SHA}"
290	            exit 1
291	          fi
292	
293	          # Get recent completed CI E2E orchestrator runs (newest first) and find
294	          # the most recent one whose commit is within the release range AND whose
295	          # Desktop E2E jobs actually ran (not skipped due to no desktop changes)
296	          RUNS=$(gh run list --repo ${{ github.repository }} \
297	            --workflow=ci-e2e.yml --branch=main --limit=50 \
298	            --json headSha,conclusion,status,databaseId \
299	            --jq '.[] | select(.status == "completed") | "\(.headSha) \(.conclusion) \(.databaseId)"')
300	
301	          LATEST_IN_RANGE_SHA=""
302	          LATEST_IN_RANGE_CONCLUSION=""
303	
304	          while IFS=" " read -r sha conclusion run_id; do
305	            if [ -z "$sha" ]; then
306	              continue
307	            fi
308	            if echo "$RANGE_COMMITS" | grep -Fq "$sha"; then
309	              if [ "$conclusion" != "success" ]; then
310	                # Found a failed run in range — report it immediately
311	                LATEST_IN_RANGE_SHA="$sha"
312	                LATEST_IN_RANGE_CONCLUSION="$conclusion"
313	                break
314	              fi
315	              # Success run — verify tests actually executed
316	              run_executed_tests "$run_id" && rc=0 || rc=$?
317	              if [ "$rc" -eq 2 ]; then
318	                exit 1
319	              elif [ "$rc" -eq 0 ]; then
320	                LATEST_IN_RANGE_SHA="$sha"
321	                LATEST_IN_RANGE_CONCLUSION="$conclusion"
322	                LATEST_IN_RANGE_RUN_ID="$run_id"
323	                break
324	              else
325	                echo "Skipping run ${run_id} at ${sha} — tests were skipped (no desktop changes detected by workflow)"
326	              fi
327	            fi
328	          done <<EOF
329	          $RUNS
330	          EOF
331	
332	          if [ -z "$LATEST_IN_RANGE_SHA" ]; then
333	            echo "::error::No Desktop E2E run found (with tests actually executed) for any commit in ${PREV_TAG}..HEAD. Desktop code changed but was never verified."
334	            exit 1
335	          fi
336	
337	          if [ "$LATEST_IN_RANGE_CONCLUSION" != "success" ]; then
338	            echo "::error::Most recent Desktop E2E run in range (at ${LATEST_IN_RANGE_SHA}) did not succeed (conclusion: ${LATEST_IN_RANGE_CONCLUSION})."
339	            exit 1
340	          fi
341	
342	          echo "Desktop E2E: passed (run ${LATEST_IN_RANGE_RUN_ID} at ${LATEST_IN_RANGE_SHA} with tests executed)"
343	
344	      - name: Skip Desktop E2E (no desktop changes)
345	        if: startsWith(github.head_ref, 'release-please--') && needs.detect-changes.outputs.desktop != 'true'
346	        run: echo "No desktop-related changes since last release — skipping Desktop E2E gate"
347	
348	      - name: Summary
349	        if: startsWith(github.head_ref, 'release-please--')
350	        run: echo "All E2E gates passed — release is safe to merge."
351	
```

> AGENT

I see the bug. On line 309, when iterating through runs in the range, the script finds a **failed** run and **immediately breaks** without checking if tests actually executed:

```bash
if [ "$conclusion" != "success" ]; then
  # Found a failed run in range — report it immediately
  LATEST_IN_RANGE_SHA="$sha"
  LATEST_IN_RANGE_CONCLUSION="$conclusion"
  break
fi
```

So run `a08414fe7` (Phase 35 TEE worker) has `conclusion: failure` — it's in the range and the script stops looking. But that run's desktop E2E tests were **skipped** (the failure was in a different job). The successful desktop E2E run at `b2f657221` is older and never reached.

The fix: for failed runs, also check if desktop tests actually executed before reporting failure. If tests were skipped, continue searching.

> AGENT

Wait — `run_executed_tests` checks for `conclusion == "success"` on the desktop jobs. For a failed run where desktop tests ran and failed, we'd also want to catch that. Let me re-read the helper:

```bash
run_executed_tests() {
  local run_id=$1
  local executed
  if ! executed=$(gh api ... --jq '[.jobs[] | select(.name | startswith("Desktop E2E")) | select(.conclusion == "success")] | length'); then
    return 2
  fi
  [ "${executed:-0}" -gt 0 ]
}
```

This only returns 0 if desktop jobs **succeeded**. A run where desktop tests ran but **failed** would return 1 (same as skipped). I need to distinguish "tests ran" from "tests succeeded". Let me update the helper:

> TOOL

tool_use Edit
id: toolu_01Ss8KGfUhvnf9YFxg2vevzu
```json
{
  "replace_all": false,
  "file_path": "/Users/michael/Code/cipher-box/.github/workflows/release-gate.yml",
  "old_string": "          # Helper: check if a workflow run actually executed desktop-e2e jobs\n          # (not skipped due to change detection). Returns 0 if tests ran, 1 if skipped, 2 on API error.\n          run_executed_tests() {\n            local run_id=$1\n            local executed\n            if ! executed=$(gh api \"repos/${{ github.repository }}/actions/runs/${run_id}/jobs\" \\\n              --jq '[.jobs[] | select(.name | startswith(\"Desktop E2E\")) | select(.conclusion == \"success\")] | length'); then\n              echo \"::error::Failed to query jobs for run ${run_id} (API error or rate limit)\"\n              return 2\n            fi\n            [ \"${executed:-0}\" -gt 0 ]\n          }",
  "new_string": "          # Helper: check if a workflow run actually executed desktop-e2e jobs\n          # (not skipped due to change detection).\n          # Returns 0 if tests ran (any conclusion), 1 if skipped/not present, 2 on API error.\n          run_executed_tests() {\n            local run_id=$1\n            local total\n            if ! total=$(gh api \"repos/${{ github.repository }}/actions/runs/${run_id}/jobs\" \\\n              --jq '[.jobs[] | select(.name | startswith(\"Desktop E2E\")) | select(.conclusion != \"skipped\" and .conclusion != null)] | length'); then\n              echo \"::error::Failed to query jobs for run ${run_id} (API error or rate limit)\"\n              return 2\n            fi\n            [ \"${total:-0}\" -gt 0 ]\n          }"
}

> AGENT

The HEAD check is fine — it first checks `CONCLUSION != "success"` exits with error, then checks if tests ran. But with the updated helper (which now detects "ran but failed"), the HEAD path needs adjustment too. If the HEAD run succeeded overall but desktop tests were skipped, it falls through correctly. If HEAD run failed, it exits with error — but that could be a non-desktop failure. Let me fix this too:

> AGENT

These are **standalone** `Desktop E2E Tests` workflow runs. But the release gate searches for `ci-e2e.yml` runs (the orchestrator), not the standalone `desktop-e2e.yml` runs:

```bash
RUNS=$(gh run list ... --workflow=ci-e2e.yml ...)
```

That's the real bug — the release gate only looks at `ci-e2e.yml` orchestrator runs for desktop E2E results, but the successful desktop tests ran as standalone `Desktop E2E Tests` workflow runs. It never finds them.

Desktop code changed but was never verified.\"\n            exit 1\n          fi\n\n          if [ \"$LATEST_IN_RANGE_CONCLUSION\" != \"success\" ]; then\n            echo \"::error::Most recent Desktop E2E run in range (at ${LATEST_IN_RANGE_SHA}) did not succeed (conclusion: ${LATEST_IN_RANGE_CONCLUSION}).\"\n            exit 1\n          fi\n\n          echo \"Desktop E2E: passed (run ${LATEST_IN_RANGE_RUN_ID} at ${LATEST_IN_RANGE_SHA} with tests executed)\"",
  "new_string": "          # Search two workflow sources for desktop E2E results:\n          # 1. ci-e2e.yml orchestrator (desktop tests as nested jobs)\n          # 2. desktop-e2e.yml standalone runs (triggered independently)\n          LATEST_IN_RANGE_SHA=\"\"\n          LATEST_IN_RANGE_CONCLUSION=\"\"\n          LATEST_IN_RANGE_RUN_ID=\"\"\n\n          # Source 1: CI E2E orchestrator runs with desktop jobs that actually executed\n          echo \"Checking ci-e2e.yml orchestrator runs...\"\n          RUNS=$(gh run list --repo ${{ github.repository }} \\\n            --workflow=ci-e2e.yml --branch=main --limit=50 \\\n            --json headSha,conclusion,status,databaseId \\\n            --jq '.[] | select(.status == \"completed\") | \"\\(.headSha) \\(.conclusion) \\(.databaseId)\"')\n\n          while IFS=\" \" read -r sha conclusion run_id; do\n            if [ -z \"$sha\" ]; then\n              continue\n            fi\n            if echo \"$RANGE_COMMITS\" | grep -Fq \"$sha\"; then\n              run_executed_tests \"$run_id\" && rc=0 || rc=$?\n              if [ \"$rc\" -eq 2 ]; then\n                exit 1\n              elif [ \"$rc\" -eq 1 ]; then\n                echo \"Skipping ci-e2e run ${run_id} at ${sha} (${conclusion}) — desktop tests skipped\"\n                continue\n              fi\n              LATEST_IN_RANGE_SHA=\"$sha\"\n              LATEST_IN_RANGE_CONCLUSION=\"$conclusion\"\n              LATEST_IN_RANGE_RUN_ID=\"$run_id\"\n              break\n            fi\n          done <<EOF\n          $RUNS\n          EOF\n\n          # Source 2: Standalone desktop-e2e.yml runs (if no ci-e2e run had desktop tests)\n          if [ -z \"$LATEST_IN_RANGE_SHA\" ]; then\n            echo \"No ci-e2e.yml run with desktop tests found. Checking standalone desktop-e2e.yml runs...\"\n            DESKTOP_RUNS=$(gh run list --repo ${{ github.repository }} \\\n              --workflow=desktop-e2e.yml --branch=main --limit=50 \\\n              --json headSha,conclusion,status,databaseId \\\n              --jq '.[] | select(.status == \"completed\") | \"\\(.headSha) \\(.conclusion) \\(.databaseId)\"')\n\n            while IFS=\" \" read -r sha conclusion run_id; do\n              if [ -z \"$sha\" ]; then\n                continue\n              fi\n              if echo \"$RANGE_COMMITS\" | grep -Fq \"$sha\"; then\n                # Standalone desktop runs always execute tests (no skip logic)\n                LATEST_IN_RANGE_SHA=\"$sha\"\n                LATEST_IN_RANGE_CONCLUSION=\"$conclusion\"\n                LATEST_IN_RANGE_RUN_ID=\"$run_id\"\n                break\n              fi\n            done <<EOF\n          $DESKTOP_RUNS\n          EOF\n          fi\n\n          if [ -z \"$LATEST_IN_RANGE_SHA\" ]; then\n            echo \"::error::No Desktop E2E run found (ci-e2e or standalone) for any commit in ${PREV_TAG}..HEAD. Desktop code changed but was never verified.\"\n            exit 1\n          fi\n\n          if [ \"$LATEST_IN_RANGE_CONCLUSION\" != \"success\" ]; then\n            echo \"::error::Most recent Desktop E2E run in range (at ${LATEST_IN_RANGE_SHA}) did not succeed (conclusion: ${LATEST_IN_RANGE_CONCLUSION}). Run: ${LATEST_IN_RANGE_RUN_ID}\"\n            exit 1\n          fi\n\n          echo \"Desktop E2E: passed (run ${LATEST_IN_RANGE_RUN_ID} at ${LATEST_IN_RANGE_SHA})\""
}

> AGENT

Pushed. To summarize what was wrong:

**The release gate only searched `ci-e2e.yml` orchestrator runs**, but the successful desktop E2E tests (runs 23697467425 and 23720651610) ran as **standalone `desktop-e2e.yml` workflows**. The orchestrator's desktop jobs were always skipped (per-commit change detection didn't detect desktop changes), so the gate never found a run with desktop tests executed.

The fix adds a two-source search: first `ci-e2e.yml` orchestrator runs (for nested desktop jobs), then falls back to standalone `desktop-e2e.yml` runs.

> AGENT

Already pushed and PR #401 updated with the standalone workflow search fix.

> DEVELOPER

is there any benefit to this approach? would it not make sense to just check the Desktop E2E jobs which would show up no matter how they were launched when trying to ensure that the desktop e2e tests ran.

> AGENT

You're right — instead of searching specific workflow files, just search for `Desktop E2E Tests` runs directly regardless of how they were triggered. Much simpler and future-proof.

> AGENT

Triaging:

1. **headSha vs actual ref** — Valid concern but doesn't apply in practice. Desktop E2E on `main` branch is only triggered by push events or ci-e2e orchestrator calls, both of which use the push SHA. `workflow_dispatch` with a different ref is a manual edge case that doesn't affect automated release gating.
2. **PR description stale** — Valid, should update the PR description.
3. **Comment/log wording mismatch** — Valid, easy fix. The intent is to find the most recent run (fail fast if it failed).
